It just... sounds completely real when it happens. But I haven't seen anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself jumping from one idea to another when talking or thinking?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I don't think so. My thoughts are just... slow, mostly. Like I said, it's just hard to think. I don't really jump around.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like the voice you hear at night is real and is actually talking to you?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. But... yeah, at the time, it feels real.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disconnected or don't make sense to you or others?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nThey just... they make sense to me, I guess. They're just slow. No shadows or... anything like that. \n\nLike I said, it's just... the sounds. The dripping, or that voice. When I check the corner with the boxes, there's never anything to see. It just... sounds completely real when it happens. But I haven't seen anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself jumping from one idea to another when talking or thinking?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I don't think so. My thoughts are just... slow, mostly. Like I said, it's just hard to think. I don't really jump around.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like the voice you hear at night is real and is actually talking to you?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I guess so. \n\nWhen it happens, it... it sounds completely real. Like I said before, it comes from that corner where my records are boxed up. It's just a low voice, saying my name. And the dripping. It sounds like it's actually happening right then, but... when I get up to look, there's just boxes. Nothing's there. But... yeah, at the time, it feels real.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disconnected or don't make sense to you or others?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nThey just... they make sense to me, I guess. They're just slow. I don't think they're disconnected.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voice you hear at night is trying to tell you something or send you a message?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I don't think so. It just... it just says my name. That's all. I don't think it means anything else.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that others have difficulty understanding what you are trying to communicate to them?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not that I've noticed. I mean... nobody has ever said they don't understand me. So... I think they get what I mean fine.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble expressing your thoughts in a way that feels coherent or logical to you?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I don't have a plan to do anything like that. It's just... I catch myself thinking sometimes that maybe it would be easier if I just didn't wake up. Just so nobody would have to deal with me anymore. But... no, I wouldn't actually hurt myself.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this heavy sadness?\n\nPatient: Um... a while, I guess. Over a year. Maybe... fourteen months? Ever since... well, a pipe burst at my old place, and I had to move into this smaller apartment. Everything just kind of... fell apart after that. My routines, I mean.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in, even if it's just a little bit?\n\nPatient: Um... not really. I mean... I wake up most days and it's just... flat. Heavy. The stuff I used to do to kind of reset myself... putting on a record, cooking... even tinkering with electronics. It just... doesn't reach me anymore. So, no. Not really.\n\nDoctor: Was the pipe bursting at your old place a very frightening or disturbing experience for you?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. It was just... a mess. Water everywhere. It wasn't scary or anything, just... a hassle. I just had to pack up and move, that's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that other people cannot?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know if it's... I mean, sometimes at night. In the new place. \n\nI'll hear... like, water dripping. Or... sometimes it sounds like a voice. Just low. Saying my name. It comes from the corner where I stacked all my boxes of records. \n\nI've gotten up to check, and... there's never anything there. No leaks, no one around. But... it sounds real when it happens. I don't really know what it is.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to concentrate or make decisions lately?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess. I don't know. Work is... harder. I just sit there sometimes, staring at the screen. It's just... hard to focus on the tickets. I don't really know about decisions. Just... hard to think.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever seen things that other people cannot, like shadows or figures, in addition to hearing the voice at night?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I don't see anything. No shadows or... anything like that. \n\nLike I said, it's just... the sounds. The dripping, or that voice. When I check the corner with the boxes, there's never anything to see. It just... sounds completely real when it happens. But I haven't seen anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself jumping from one idea to another when talking or thinking?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I don't think so. My thoughts are just... slow, mostly. Like I said, it's just hard to think. I don't really jump around.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like the voice you hear at night is real and is actually talking to you?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I guess so. \n\nWhen it happens, it... it sounds completely real. Like I said before, it comes from that corner where my records are boxed up. It's just a low voice, saying my name. And the dripping. It sounds like it's actually happening right then, but... when I get up to look, there's just boxes. Nothing's there. But... yeah, at the time, it feels real.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disconnected or don't make sense to you or others?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nThey just... they make sense to me, I guess. They're just slow. I don't think they're disconnected.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voice you hear at night is trying to tell you something or send you a message?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I don't think so. It just... it just says my name. That's all. I don't think it means anything else.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that others have difficulty understanding what you are trying to communicate to them?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not that I've noticed. I mean... nobody has ever said they don't understand me. So... I think they get what I mean fine.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself having trouble expressing your thoughts in a way that feels coherent or logical to you?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I usually know what I'm trying to say. I just... I don't always feel like talking much right now. But it's logical to me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing the voice at night?\n\nPatient: Um... about a year and two months, I think. It started... well, a pipe burst forced me to move into a smaller apartment. My routines just... kind of fell apart. So, since around then.\n\nDoctor: Have the voice and sounds you hear at night been constant over the past year or have they come and gone?\n\nPatient: Um... they come and go. I never had those voices before... or that fear about being watched at work. Not before the move, anyway, before the depression set in. They just... they show up during the bad stretches. Like when I'm feeling really low... or guilty and just worn down. When I'm doing okay, they aren't there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voices and sounds you hear at night started after you began feeling the heavy sadness?\n\nPatient: Yeah... like I was just saying. I didn't have any of that before the move. The heavy feeling definitely started first. The voices just... they only really happen when I'm already feeling low and worn out.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time where the voices or sounds you heard at night were present for most of every day?\n\nPatient: No... not most of the day. Just... just at night, like I said. When things are quiet.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"
  },
